 33→params.condition(end+1) = {'hit&~stim.enable&autowater&~early'};                % all AW hits, ~early,no stim\n    34→\n    35→params.tmin = -3;       % min time (in s) relative to alignEvent \n    36→params.tmax = 2.5;      % max time (in s) relative to alignEvent \n    37→params.dt = 1/100;      % bin size (s)\n    38→\n    39→% smooth with causal gaussian kernel\n    40→params.smooth = 15;\n    41→\n    42→% Sorted unit qualities to use (can specify whether you only want to use\n    43→% single units or multi units)\n    44→params.quality = {'all'}; % accepts any cell array of strings - special character 'all' returns clusters of any quality\n    45→\n    46→% Kinematic features that you want to load\n    47→params.traj_features = {{'tongue','left_tongue','right_tongue','jaw','trident','nose'},...\n    48→    {'top_tongue','topleft_tongue','bottom_tongue','bottomleft_tongue','jaw','top_nostril','bottom_nostril'}};\n    49→\n    50→params.feat_varToExplain = 80; % num factors for dim reduction of video features should explain this much variance\n    51→\n    52→params.N_varToExplain = 80; % keep num dims that explains this much variance in neural data (when doing n/p)\n    53→\n    54→params.advance_movement = 0;\n    55→\n    56→params.bctype = 'reflect'; % options are : reflect  zeropad  none\n    57→%% SPECIFY DATA TO LOAD\n    58→\n    59→datapth = 'C:\\Users\\Jackie Birnbaum\\Documents\\Data';\n    60→\n    61→meta = [];\n    62→\n    63→% Scripts for loading data from each animal \n    64→meta = loadJEB6_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    65→meta = loadJEB7_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    66→meta = loadEKH1_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    67→meta = loadEKH3_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    68→meta = loadJGR2_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    69→meta = loadJGR3_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    70→meta = loadJEB19_ALMVideo(meta,datapth);\n    71→\n    72→params.probe = {meta.probe}; % put probe numbers into params, one entry for element in meta, just so i don't have to change code i've already written\n    73→%% LOAD DATA\n    74→\n    75→% ----------------------------------------------\n    76→% -- Neural Data --\n    77→% obj (struct array) - one entry per session\n    78→% params (struct array) - one entry per session\n    79→% ----------------------------------------------\n    80→[obj,params] = loadSessionData(meta,params);\n    81→%%\n    82→% ------------------------------------------\n    83→% -- Motion Energy --\n    84→% me (struct array) - one entry per session\n    85→% ------------------------------------------\n    86→for sessix = 1:numel(meta)\n    87→    me(sessix) = loadMotionEnergy(obj(sessix), meta(sessix), params(sessix), datapth);\n    88→end\n    89→%% Load kinematic data\n    90→nSessions = numel(meta);\n    91→for sessix = 1:numel(meta)\n    92→    message = strcat('----Getting kinematic data for session',{' '},num2str(sessix), {' '},'out of',{' '},num2str(nSessions),'----');\n    93→    disp(message)\n    94→    kin(sessix) = getKinematics(obj(sessix), me(sessix), params(sessix));\n    95→end\n    96→%% Find Movement-null and Movement-potent subspaces using all trials\n    97→clearvars -except obj meta params me sav kin\n    98→\n    99→% -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n   100→% -- Curate Input Data --\n   101→% zscore single trial neural data (time*trials,neurons), for all trials\n   102→% -- Calculate null and potent spaces --\n   103→% null space from quiet time points\n   104→% potent space from moving time points\n   105→% -----------------------------------------------------------------------\n   106→for sessix = 1:numel(meta)      % For each session...  \n   107→    % -- input data\n   108→    trialdat_zscored = zscore_singleTrialNeuralData(obj(sessix));\n   109→    zscored(sessix).trialdat =  trialdat_zscored;\n   110→\n   111→    % -- Calculate the movement-null and movement-potent spaces for each session\n   112→    cond2use = [2 3 4 5];   % All DR hit/miss trials, all WC hit/miss trials (NUMBERING ACCORDING TO PARAMS.CONDITION)\n   113→    nullalltime = 0;        % use all time points to estimate movement-null space if 1\n   114→    WConly = 0;             % use only WC to find null and potent spaces \n   115→    delayOnly = 0;          % use only delay period to find null and potent spaces\n   116→    cond2proj = [6 7];      % Conditions to project into null and potent spaces (NUMBERING ACCORDING TO PARAMS.CONDITION)\n   117→    rez(sessix) = singleTrial_elsayed_np(trialdat_zscored, obj(sessix), me(sessix), params(sessix), cond2use, cond2proj,nullalltime,WConly,delayOnly); \n   118→end\n   119→%% Split trials into Move vs. Non-Move (based on motion energy during the \n   120→% presample period)\n   121→\n   122→%%% Time parameters %%%\n   123→% Specify times in which you want to find move/non-move trials\n   124→times.trialstart = median(obj(1).bp.ev.bitStart)-median(obj(1).bp.ev.(params(1).alignEvent));\n   125→times.startix = find(obj(1).time>times.trialstart,1,'first');\n   126→times.samp = median(obj(1).bp.ev.sample)-median(obj(1).bp.ev.(params(1).alignEvent));\n   127→times.stopix = find(obj(1).time<times.samp,1,'last');\n   128→times.go = median(obj(1).bp.ev.goCue)-median(obj(1).bp.ev.(params(1).alignEvent));\n   129→times.goix = find(obj(1).time<times.go,1,'last');\n   130→\n   131→%%% Trial parameters %%%\n   132→cond2use = [6 7];           % DR, WC -- Conditions that you want to use (with reference to params.condition)                                                         \n   133→trials2cutoff = 40;         % Trials to cut-off at the end of the session (because animals tend to have a very high no response rate at the end of the session before we cut it off)\n   134→\n   135→%%% Find move vs. non-move trials %%%\n   136→% MoveNonMove = (1 x nSessions) struct with fields 'noMove' and 'Move'\n   137→% Each field has subfields 'DR' and 'WC'\n   138→% 'DR' and 'WC' are (n x 1) where 'n' = the number of move or non-move trials in that context\n   139→MoveNonMove = findMoveNonMoveTrix(meta, obj, trials2cutoff, cond2use, params, me, times);\n   140→\n   141→clearvars -except obj meta params rez zscored me MoveNonMove times kin\n   142→%% Get motion energy on move vs non-move trials\n   143→contexts = {'DR','WC'};                 % Trial contexts {DR, WC}\n   144→movecont = {'noMove','Move','all'};      % Movement condition\n   145→\n   146→sm = 30;\n   147→kinfeat = \"motion_energy\";\n   148→featix = find(strcmp(kin(1).featLeg,kinfeat));\n   149→\n   150→%%% Group trials from both contexts %%%\n   151→avgME.noMove.both = NaN(length(obj(1).time),length(obj));\n   152→avgME.Move.both = NaN(length(obj(1).time),length(obj));\n   153→for sessix = 1:length(meta)\n   154→    for mm = 1:numel(movecont)          % For 'move', 'non-move', and 'all' trials...\n   155→        currmove = movecont{mm};\n   156→        trix2use = [];\n   157→        % Concatenate trials from DR and WC\n   158→        for cc = 1:numel(contexts)\n   159→            currcont = contexts{cc};\n   160→            currtrix = MoveNonMove(sessix).(currmove).(currcont);\n   161→            trix2use = [trix2use;currtrix];\n   162→        end\n   163→        % Get the avg ME across trials for move vs non-move trial\n   164→        currME = squeeze(kin(sessix).dat(:,trix2use,featix));       % [time x trials]\n   165→        currME = mean(currME,2,'omitnan');                          % [time x 1]\n   166→        avgME.(currmove).both(:,sessix) = mySmooth(currME,sm,'reflect');    % Smooth and save for this session and movement category\n   167→\n   168→    end\n   169→end\n   170→%% Figure 8b, bottom: Plot avg ME on move vs non-move trials\n   171→colors = getColors();\n   172→\n   173→alph = 0.2;                 % Opacity parameter\n   174→xl = [-2.4 0];              % Xlimits for plot (time)\n   175→nSessions = length(meta);\n   176→figure();\n   177→\n   178→% Plot average ME + confidence intervals for 'no-move' trials (dashed line)\n   179→col = [0 0 0];\n   180→toplot = mean(avgME.noMove.both,2,'omitnan');\n   181→err = 1.96*(std(avgME.noMove.both,0,2,'omitnan') ./ sqrt(nSessions));\n   182→ax = gca;\n   183→shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2.5,'LineStyle','--'},alph,ax); hold on;\n   184→\n   185→% Plot average ME + confidence intervals for 'move' trials (solid line)\n   186→toplot = mean(avgME.Move.both,2,'omitnan');\n   187→err = 1.96*(std(avgME.Move.both,0,2,'omitnan') ./ sqrt(nSessions));\n   188→ax = gca;\n   189→shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2},alph,ax); hold on;\n   190→\n   191→xline(0,'k--')\n   192→xline(-2.2,'k--')\n   193→xline(-0.9,'k--')\n   194→xlim(xl)\n   195→ylim([0 32])\n   196→ylabel('Motion energy (a.u.)')\n   197→set(gca,'TickDir','out')\n   198→title('Motion energy - both contexts')\n   199→%% Get average ME during presample period on move vs non-move trials\n   200→% Time parameters\n   201→ix1 = find(obj(1).time>times.trialstart,1,'first');\n   202→ix2 = find(obj(1).time<times.samp,1,'last');\n   203→% 'Move' trials\n   204→temp = mean(avgME.Move.both(ix1:ix2,:),1,'omitnan');    % [1 x nSessions]\n   205→avgITI_ME.Move = mean(temp,'omitnan');                  % Avg value across sessions\n   206→% 'Non-move' trials\n   207→temp = mean(avgME.noMove.both(ix1:ix2,:),1,'omitnan');\n   208→avgITI_ME.noMove = mean(temp,'omitnan');\n   209→\n   210→% Find percent reduction in motion energy \n   211→MEratio = avgITI_ME.noMove/avgITI_ME.Move;              \n   212→MEreduct = 100*(1-MEratio);\n   213→\n   214→disp('---Reduction in Motion energy, Move vs noMove---')\n   215→disp(['Average ITI motion energy on MOVE trials =  ' num2str(avgITI_ME.Move)])\n   216→disp(['Average ITI motion energy on QUIET trials =  ' num2str(avgITI_ME.noMove)])\n   217→disp(['Reduction in ITI motion energy = ' num2str(MEreduct)])\n   218→%% Calculate CDcontext\n   219→% CDcontext calculated in full population and both subspaces (not separated by move/non-move)\n   220→% Trials used for projections onto CDcontext are held out test trials (separated by move/non-move)\n   221→\n   222→condfns = {'DR','WC'};                 % Trial contexts {DR, WC}\n   223→popfns = {'null','potent','fullpop'};   % Subspaces (mov-null, mov-potent, no decomposition)\n   224→movefns = {'noMove','Move','all'};      % Movement condition\n   225→\n   226→% New field added to 'cd_null' = 'testsingleproj'\n   227→% testsingleproj has fields 'noMove', 'Move', 'all' indicating whether these single trial projections are from noMove trials, move trials, or all trials\n   228→% Each field then has a (1 x 2) cell array.  First cell contains single test trial projections from the DR context. \n   229→% Second cell contains projections from WC context\n   230→[cd_null, cd_potent, cd_context] = CDContext_AllSpaces_NonStation(obj,meta,rez,popfns,condfns,movefns,MoveNonMove,params,zscored);\n   231→\n   232→clearvars -except cd_context cd_null cd_potent obj params meta me rez zscored testsplit nSplits times\n   233→%% Reorganize into a simpler structure\n   234→% Grouped = (1 x nSessions) struct with fields 'fullpop', 'null', 'potent'\n   235→% Each field has subfields 'noMove','Move','all'\n   236→% 'noMove','Move', and 'all' are (1 x 2) cell. Where first cell contains single trial projections onto CDContext for DR trials (of that move condition)\n   237→% and second cell contains for WC trials\n   238→\n   239→popfns = {'fullpop','null','potent'};\n   240→movefns = {'noMove','Move','all'};\n   241→\n   242→grouped = reorganizeMnM(meta, popfns, movefns, cd_context, cd_null, cd_potent);\n   243→\n   244→clearvars -except cd_context cd_null cd_potent obj params meta me rez zscored testsplit times grouped popfns movefns\n   245→%% Group across all sessions\n   246→% Find average CDContext in all move conditions and task conditions\n   247→% Find selectivity (btw DR and AW)\n   248→\n   249→% all_grouped = (1 x nSessions) struct with fields 'fullpop', 'null', 'potent'\n   250→% Each field has subfields 'noMove','Move','all'\n   251→% 'noMove','Move', and 'all' are (1 x 2) cell. Where first cell contains single trial projections onto CDContext for DR trials (of that move condition)\n   252→% and second cell contains for WC trials\n   253→sm=60;\n   254→all_grouped = combineSessions_grouped(meta,grouped,sm, popfns, movefns);\n   255→%% Figure 8a: Plot average CDContext (+ confidence intervals) across all sessions for each context\n   256→\n   257→colors = getColors();\n   258→alph = 0.2;             % Shading opacity for error bars\n   259→condfns = {'afc','aw'}; % 'afc' = DR trials; 'aw' = WC\n   260→nSessions = length(meta);\n   261→figure();\n   262→\n   263→%%% Full population -- no subspace decomposition %%%\n   264→subplot(3,1,1)\n   265→for cond = 1:length(condfns)\n   266→    if cond ==1\n   267→        col = colors.afc;\n   268→    else\n   269→        col = colors.aw;\n   270→    end\n   271→    toplot = mean(mySmooth(all_grouped.fullpop.all.(condfns{cond}),60),2,'omitnan');\n   272→    err = 1.96*(std(mySmooth(all_grouped.fullpop.all.(condfns{cond}),60),0,2,'omitnan') ./ sqrt(nSessions));\n   273→    ax = gca;\n   274→    shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2},alph,ax); hold on;\n   275→end\n   276→xline(-2.2,'k--')\n   277→xline(-0.9,'k--')\n   278→xline(-0,'k--')\n   279→xlabel('Time from go cue / water drop (s)')\n   280→ylabel('a.u.')\n   281→title('Full pop')\n   282→xlim([-2.5 2.5])\n   283→set(gca,'TickDir','out');\n   284→\n   285→%%% Null space %%%\n   286→subplot(3,1,2)\n   287→for cond = 1:length(condfns)\n   288→    if cond ==1\n   289→        col = colors.afc;\n   290→    else\n   291→        col = colors.aw;\n   292→    end\n   293→    toplot = mean(mySmooth(all_grouped.null.all.(condfns{cond}),60),2,'omitnan');\n   294→    err = 1.96*(std(mySmooth(all_grouped.null.all.(condfns{cond}),60),0,2,'omitnan') ./ sqrt(nSessions));\n   295→    ax = gca;\n   296→    shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2},alph,ax); hold on;\n   297→end\n   298→xline(-2.2,'k--')\n   299→xline(-0.9,'k--')\n   300→xline(-0,'k--')\n   301→xlabel('Time from go cue / water drop (s)')\n   302→ylabel('a.u.')\n   303→title('Null')\n   304→xlim([-2.5 2.5])\n   305→set(gca,'TickDir','out');\n   306→\n   307→%%% Potent space %%%\n   308→subplot(3,1,3)\n   309→for cond = 1:length(condfns)\n   310→    if cond ==1\n   311→        col = colors.afc;\n   312→    else\n   313→        col = colors.aw;\n   314→    end\n   315→    ax = gca;\n   316→    toplot = mean(mySmooth(all_grouped.potent.all.(condfns{cond}),60),2,'omitnan');\n   317→    err = 1.96*(std(mySmooth(all_grouped.potent.all.(condfns{cond}),60),0,2,'omitnan') ./ sqrt(nSessions));\n   318→    shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2},alph,ax); hold on;\n   319→end\n   320→xline(-2.2,'k--')\n   321→xline(-0.9,'k--')\n   322→xline(-0,'k--')\n   323→xlabel('Time from go cue / water drop (s)')\n   324→ylabel('a.u.')\n   325→title('Potent')\n   326→xlim([-2.5 2.5])\n   327→set(gca,'TickDir','out');\n   328→%% Figure 8b: Plot average selectivity in CDcontext across all sessions for each context\n   329→LinePlot_SelGrouped_MoveNonMove_V2(meta,all_grouped,times,alph,colors,obj,movefns,popfns)\n   330→%% Get the average presample selectivity in CDcont for move and non-move trials\n   331→for ii = 1:length(popfns)           % For each subspace...\n   332→    cont = popfns{ii};\n   333→    for gg = 1:length(movefns)      % For each movement condition...\n   334→        presampavg.(cont).(movefns{gg}) = mean(all_grouped.(cont).(movefns{gg}).selectivity(times.startix:times.stopix,:),1,'omitnan');\n   335→    end\n   336→end\n   337→%% Normalize the selectivity to the max presample selectivity in each \"condition\" i.e. fullpop, null, potent\n   338→for ii = 1:length(popfns)\n   339→    cont = popfns{ii};\n   340→    temp = [presampavg.(cont).noMove, presampavg.(cont).Move];              % Take all presamp average values for CDCont, in move vs non-move\n   341→    maxsel = max(abs(temp));                                                % Take the maximum of the absolute value of these\n   342→   \n   343→    presampavgnorm.(cont).noMove = abs(presampavg.(cont).noMove)./maxsel;   % Normalize all presamp avgs from this condition to this value \n   344→    presampavgnorm.(cont).Move = abs(presampavg.(cont).Move)./maxsel;       % For Move as well\n   345→end\n   346→%% Get reduction in context selectivity in each subspace\n   347→\n   348→for ii = 1:length(popfns)           % For each subspace...\n   349→    cont = popfns{ii}; \n   350→\n   351→    % Reduction in selectivity values\n   352→    HLdelt = presampavg.(cont).Move - presampavg.(cont).noMove;\n   353→    ReductVals.mean.(cont) = mean(HLdelt,'omitnan');\n   354→    ReductVals.std.(cont) = std(HLdelt,'omitnan');\n   355→end\n   356→\n   357→%%% Summary statistics %%%\n   358→disp('---Raw reduction vals in selectivity from move to non-move trials---')\n   359→disp(['Full pop =  ' num2str(ReductVals.mean.fullpop) ' +/- ' num2str(ReductVals.std.fullpop)])\n   360→disp(['Null =  ' num2str(ReductVals.mean.null) ' +/- ' num2str(ReductVals.std.null)])\n   361→disp(['Potent =  ' num2str(ReductVals.mean.potent) ' +/- ' num2str(ReductVals.std.potent)])\n   362→t = datetime('now','TimeZone','local','Format','d-MMM-y HH:mm:ss Z');\n   363→disp(t)\n   364→\n   365→%% Do all two-sided t-tests (paired) -- between move and non-move of the same subspace \n   366→sigcutoff = 0.01;\n   367→allhyp = [];            % (3 x 1).  First row = full pop.  Second row = null. Third row = potent.             \n   368→for ii = 1:length(popfns)\n   369→    cont = popfns{ii};  \n   370→    [hyp.(cont),pval.(cont)] = ttest(presampavg.(cont).noMove,presampavg.(cont).Move,'Alpha',sigcutoff);\n   371→end\n   372→disp('---Summary Statistics for average ITI context selectivity---')\n   373→disp(['For significance cutoff ' num2str(sigcutoff) ' :'])\n   374→pval\n   375→disp(['Nsessions = ' num2str(length(meta))])\n   376→t = datetime('now','TimeZone','local','Format','d-MMM-y HH:mm:ss Z');\n   377→disp(t)\n   378→%% Do all t-tests (paired) -- between deltas (move - low move) in null vs potent\n   379→sigcutoff = 0.01;\n   380→allhyp = [];            % (3 x 1).  First row = full pop.  Second row = null. Third row = potent.             \n   381→\n   382→for ii = 1:2\n   383→    if ii ==1\n   384→        pop = 'null';\n   385→    else\n   386→        pop = 'potent';\n   387→    end\n   388→    delt.(pop) = presampavg.(pop).Move - presampavg.(pop).noMove;\n   389→end\n   390→\n   391→[hyp.delta.NullPot,pval.delta.NullPot] = ttest(delt.null,delt.potent,'Alpha',sigcutoff);\n   392→\n   393→disp('---paired t-test comparing deltas (move - no move) in null vs potent---')\n   394→disp(['For significance cutoff ' num2str(sigcutoff) ' :'])\n   395→pval.delta.NullPot\n   396→disp(['Nsessions = ' num2str(length(meta))])\n   397→t = datetime('now','TimeZone','local','Format','d-MMM-y HH:mm:ss Z');\n   398→disp(t)\n   399→%% Figure 8c: bar plot + scatter plot of avg presample CDContext on move trials vs non-move trials\n   400→% Xtick marks: [1,2] = full pop; [3,4] = null space; [5,6] = potent space\n   401→% Odd numbers = 'Move' trials; Even numbers = 'Non-move' trials \n   402→plotBarPlot_Scatter_WLines(presampavg,meta)\n   403→%% Plotting functions\n   404→\n   405→function LinePlot_SelGrouped_MoveNonMove_V2(meta,all_grouped,times,alph,colors,obj,movefns,popfns)\n   406→nSessions = length(meta);\n   407→cnt = 1;\n   408→for po = 1:length(popfns)        % For each subspace...\n   409→    cont = popfns{po};\n   410→    switch cont\n   411→        case 'fullpop'\n   412→        yl = [-0.05 0.255];\n   413→        col = [0.25 0.25 0.25];\n   414→        case 'null'\n   415→        yl = [-0.05 0.255];\n   416→        col = colors.null;\n   417→        case 'potent'\n   418→        yl = [-0.05 0.255];\n   419→        col = colors.potent;\n   420→    end\n   421→    % Plot the selectivity across all trials\n   422→    subplot(3,2,cnt)\n   423→    ax = gca;\n   424→    toplot = mean(all_grouped.(cont).all.selectivity,2,'omitnan');\n   425→    err = std(all_grouped.(cont).all.selectivity,0,2,'omitnan')./sqrt(nSessions);\n   426→    shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2},alph,ax);\n   427→    ylim(yl)\n   428→    xline(times.samp,'k--','LineWidth',1)\n   429→    xlim([-2.4 0])\n   430→    ylabel(cont)\n   431→    set(gca,'TickDir','out');\n   432→    title('All trials')\n   433→    cnt = cnt+1;\n   434→\n   435→    % Plot the selectivity on move trials and non-move trials\n   436→    for gg = 1:2\n   437→        if gg==2                            % Move trials\n   438→            style = '-';\n   439→            alp = alph;\n   440→        elseif gg==1                        % Non-Move trials\n   441→            style = '--';\n   442→            alp = alph-0.1;\n   443→        end\n   444→\n   445→        subplot(3,2,cnt)\n   446→        ax = gca;\n   447→        toplot = mean(all_grouped.(cont).(movefns{gg}).selectivity,2,'omitnan');\n   448→        err = std(all_grouped.(cont).(movefns{gg}).selectivity,0,2,'omitnan')./sqrt(nSessions);\n   449→        shadedErrorBar(obj(1).time,toplot,err,{'Color',col,'LineWidth',2,'LineStyle',style},alp,ax);\n   450→        hold on;\n   451→\n   452→        ylim(yl)\n   453→        xline(times.samp,'k--','LineWidth',1)\n   454→        xlim([-2.4 0])\n   455→        set(gca,'TickDir','out');\n   456→        ylabel('Selectivity (a.u.)')\n   457→        legend({movefns{1} movefns{2}})\n   458→    end\n   459→    cnt = cnt+1;\n   460→end\n   461→end\n   462→\n   463→function DATA LOADING - loadObjs.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/loadObjs.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction obj = loadObjs(meta)\nfor i = 1:numel(meta)\n    dat = load(meta(i).datapth);\n    if ~isfield(dat.obj,'meta')\n        dat.obj.meta = 'assigning to avoid dissimilar struct error';\n    end\n    if ~isfield(dat.obj,'ex')\n        dat.obj.ex = 'assigning to avoid dissimilar struct error';\n    end\n    if ~isfield(dat.obj,'me')\n        dat.obj.me = 'assigning to avoid dissimilar struct error';\n    end\n    obj(i) = dat.obj;\n    clear dat\nend\nend\n```\n\nSimple loader that:\n- Loads each .mat file specified in `meta.datapth`\n- Assigns placeholder fields if missing to avoid struct dissimilarity errors\n- Returns array of loaded data objects\n\n---\n\n## 3. TRIAL FILTERING - findTrials.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/findTrials.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction trialNums = findTrials(obj, conditions)\n\nvarnames = getStructVarNames(obj);\nNtrials = obj.bp.Ntrials;\nfor i = 1:numel(varnames)\n    eval([varnames{i} ' = obj.bp.' varnames{i} ';']);\n    \n    if eval(['numel(' varnames{i} ')==obj.bp.Ntrials && isrow(' varnames{i} ')'])\n        eval([varnames{i} '=' varnames{i} ''';']);\n    end\n    \n    eval([varnames{i} '=' varnames{i} '(~isnan(' varnames{i} '))' ''';'])\nend\n\nif isfield (obj.bp, 'fidx')\n    obj.bp.Ntrials = sum(obj.bp.fidx==2);\n    addToTrialNums = sum(obj.bp.fidx==1);\nend\n\nmask = zeros(obj.bp.Ntrials, numel(conditions));\n\nfor i = 1:numel(conditions)\n    try\n        mask(:,i) = eval(conditions{i});\n    catch\n        mask(:,i) = eval(conditions{i}{1});\n    end\n    if exist('addToTrialNums','var') == 1\n        trialNums{i} = find(mask(:,i)) + addToTrialNums;\n    else\n        trialNums{i} = find(mask(:,i));\n    end\nend\nend\n```\n\nFilters trials by dynamically evaluating condition strings. The function:\n- Extracts all trial variables from `obj.bp` using `getStructVarNames`\n- Removes NaN values from each variable\n- Evaluates condition strings (e.g., `'R&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'`)\n- Handles older data with `fidx` field (first vs. second half of recording)\n- Returns cell array of trial indices for each condition\n\n**Typical conditions from getDefaultParams.m:**\n```matlab\nparams.condition(1) = {'R&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'};      % right hits, no stim, aw off\nparams.condition(2) = {'L&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'};      % left hits, no stim, aw off\nparams.condition(3) = {'R&hit&~stim.enable&autowater&~early'};       % right hits, no stim, aw on\nparams.condition(4) = {'L&hit&~stim.enable&autowater&~early'};       % left hits, no stim, aw on\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. CLUSTER FILTERING - findClusters.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/findClusters.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction idx = findClusters(qualityList, qualities)\n% find idx where qualityList contains at least one of the patterns in\n% qualities\n\n% remove leading/trailing spaces\n% also check if each entry in qualityList is a string/char, if not, change it\nfor i = 1:numel(qualityList)\n    if ~ischar(qualityList{i}) && ~isstring(qualityList{i})\n        qualityList{i} = '';\n    end\nend\nqualityList = strtrim(qualityList);\n\nif any(strcmpi(qualities,'all')) || strcmpi(qualities{1},'all')\n    idx = find(~ismember(qualityList,'garbage') & ~ismember(qualityList,'gabrga') & ~ismember(qualityList,'noisy') & ~ismember(qualityList,'real?'));\n    return\nend\n\n% handle unlabeled cluster qualities\nfor i = 1:numel(qualityList)\n    if isempty(qualityList{i})\n        qualityList(i) = {'nan'};\n    end\nend\n\n[~,mask] = patternMatchCellArray(qualityList, qualities, 'any');\n\nidx = find(mask);\nend\n```\n\nFilters clusters by quality label. When `qualities = {'all'}`:\n- **EXCLUDES:** 'garbage', 'gabrga', 'noisy', 'real?'\n- **INCLUDES:** all other labels and unlabeled clusters\n\nThis is called in processData as:\n```matlab\nparams.cluid{prbnum} = findClusters({obj.clu{prbnum}(:).quality}', params.quality);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. SPIKE ALIGNMENT - alignSpikes.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/alignSpikes.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction obj = alignSpikes(obj,params,prbnum)\n\nif strcmp(params.alignEvent,'moveOnset')\n    obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent) = findMoveOnset(obj);\nend\n\nif strcmp(params.alignEvent,'firstLick')\n    % get first lick time for left and right licks\n    temp = obj.bp.ev.lickL;\n    idx = ~cellfun('isempty',temp);\n    outL = zeros(size(temp));\n    outL(idx) = cellfun(@(v)v(1),temp(idx));\n    temp = obj.bp.ev.lickR;\n    idx = ~cellfun('isempty',temp);\n    outR = zeros(size(temp));\n    outR(idx) = cellfun(@(v)v(1),temp(idx));\n    firstLick = zeros(size(temp));\n    % firstLick = min(outL,outR), except when outL||outR == 0\n    outL(outL==0) = nan;\n    outR(outR==0) = nan;\n    firstLick = nanmin(outL,outR);\n    firstLick(isnan(firstLick)) = 0;\n    obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent) = firstLick;\nend\n\nif strcmp(params.alignEvent,'lastLick')\n    % get last lick time for left and right licks\n    temp = obj.bp.ev.lickL;\n    idx = ~cellfun('isempty',temp);\n    outL = zeros(size(temp));\n    outL(idx) = cellfun(@(v)v(end),temp(idx));\n    temp = obj.bp.ev.lickR;\n    idx = ~cellfun('isempty',temp);\n    outR = zeros(size(temp));\n    outR(idx) = cellfun(@(v)v(end),temp(idx));\n    lastLick = zeros(size(temp));\n    % lastLick = max(outL,outR), except when outL||outR == 0\n    outL(outL==0) = nan;\n    outR(outR==0) = nan;\n    lastLick = nanmax(outL,outR);\n    lastLick(isnan(lastLick)) = 0;\n    obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent) = lastLick;\nend\n\nif strcmp(params.alignEvent,'jawOnset')\n    % half rise time to first peak of jaw onset after go cue\n    view = 1; % side cam\n    [~,jawMask] = patternMatchCellArray(obj.traj{view}(1).featNames,{'jaw'},'all');\n    feat = find(jawMask); % jaw\n    \n    % filter params\n    opts.f_cut = 60; % cutoff freq for butter filt\n    opts.f_n   = 2;  % filter order\n    \n    % peak finding params\n    opts.minpkdist = 0.06; % number of ms around peaks to reject peaks\n    opts.minpkprom = 10;   % a threshold for the peak size\n    obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent) = alignJawOnset(view,feat,obj,opts);\nend\n\n% align spikes to params.alignEvent\nfor clu = 1:numel(obj.clu{prbnum})\n    event = obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent)(obj.clu{prbnum}(clu).trial);\n    obj.clu{prbnum}(clu).trialtm_aligned = obj.clu{prbnum}(clu).trialtm - event;\nend\nend\n```\n\nAligns spike times to specified event. Default is 'goCue' but supports:\n- `'goCue'` - trial start signal\n- `'moveOnset'` - computed movement onset\n- `'firstLick'` - earliest lick (L or R)\n- `'lastLick'` - latest lick (L or R)\n- `'jawOnset'` - jaw onset based on video\n\nFor each cluster, subtracts the event time from each spike's trial time, storing result in `trialtm_aligned`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. SPIKE BINNING & PSTH - getSeq.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/getSeq.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction obj = getSeq(obj,params,prbnum,varargin)\n\nif isfield(obj,'psth')\n    obj = rmfield(obj,{'psth','trialdat'});\nend\n\nif nargin > 3\n    doPSTH = varargin{1};\nelse\n    doPSTH = true;\nend\n\nedges = params.tmin:params.dt:params.tmax;\nobj.time = edges + params.dt/2;\nobj.time = obj.time(1:end-1);\n\nspkedges = params.tmin:0.001:params.tmax;\n\n% get psths by condition\nobj.psth{prbnum} = zeros(numel(obj.time),numel(params.cluid{prbnum}),numel(params.condition));\nif doPSTH\n    for i = 1:numel(params.cluid{prbnum})\n        curClu = params.cluid{prbnum}(i);\n        for j = 1:numel(params.condition)\n\n            trix = params.trialid{j};\n\n            spkix = ismember(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trial, trix);\n\n            % if no spikes found for current set of trials (trix), move on to next set\n            if all(~spkix)\n                continue\n            end\n\n            N = histc(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trialtm_aligned(spkix), edges);\n            N = N(1:end-1);\n\n            obj.psth{prbnum}(:,i,j) = mySmooth(N./numel(trix)./params.dt, params.smooth, params.bctype);\n        end\n    end\nend\n\n% get single trial data\nobj.trialdat{prbnum} = zeros(numel(obj.time),numel(params.cluid{prbnum}),obj.bp.Ntrials);\nfor i = 1:numel(params.cluid{prbnum})\n    curClu = params.cluid{prbnum}(i);\n    for j = 1:obj.bp.Ntrials\n\n        spkix = ismember(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trial, j);\n\n        % if no spikes found for current trial (j), move on to next trial\n        if all(~spkix)\n            continue\n        end\n\n        N = histc(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trialtm_aligned(spkix), edges);\n        N = N(1:end-1);\n        if size(N,2) > size(N,1)\n            N = N'; % make sure N is a column vector\n        end\n\n        obj.trialdat{prbnum}(:,i,j) = mySmooth(N./params.dt,params.smooth, params.bctype);\n    end\nend\nend\n```\n\nKey parameters from default settings:\n```matlab\nparams.tmin = -2.5;              % start time (s), relative to alignment event\nparams.tmax = 2.5;               % end time (s), relative to alignment event\nparams.dt = 1/200;               % bin size = 5 ms\nparams.smooth = 15;              % gaussian kernel window for smoothing\nparams.bctype = 'none';          % boundary condition type for smoothing\n```\n\nBins and smooths neural data:\n- Creates time bins: `edges = -2.5:0.005:2.5`\n- Counts spikes per bin using `histc`\n- Converts to firing rate: `count / (num_trials * dt)` for PSTHs\n- Normalizes by dt for single trials: `count / dt`\n- Applies causal Gaussian smoothing with kernel window N=15\n- Returns arrays: `psth(time x neuron x condition)` and `trialdat(time x neuron x trial)`\n\n---\n\n## 7. LOW FIRING RATE REMOVAL - removeLowFRClusters.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/removeLowFRClusters.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [obj,cluid] = removeLowFRClusters(obj,cluid,lowFR,prbnum)\n% Remove low-firing rate units, e.g., all those firing less than lowFR\n% spikes per second on average across all trials.\n%\n% The fitted observation noise (diagonal element of R) for a\n% low-firing rate unit will be small, so the neural trajectory may\n% show a deflection each time the neuron spikes.\n\nmeanFRs = mean(mean(obj.psth{prbnum},3,'omitnan'),'omitnan');\nuse = meanFRs > lowFR;\n\n% remove low fr clusters\ncluid = cluid(use);\nobj.psth{prbnum} = obj.psth{prbnum}(:,use,:);\nobj.trialdat{prbnum} = obj.trialdat{prbnum}(:,use,:);\nend\n```\n\nDefault threshold from getDefaultParams:\n```matlab\nparams.lowFR = 0.5;  % remove clusters with firing rates across all trials less than this val (spikes/sec)\n```\n\nRemoves neurons with mean firing rate < 0.5 Hz across all trials by:\n- Computing mean FR: average across time and conditions\n- Identifying low-FR neurons: `meanFRs > lowFR`\n- Removing them from cluster list and data arrays\n\n---\n\n## 8. MOTION ENERGY LOADING - loadMotionEnergy.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/loadMotionEnergy.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction me = loadMotionEnergy(obj,meta,params,datapth,varargin)\n\nif nargin > 4\n    returnAfterLoad = varargin{1};\nelse\n    returnAfterLoad = 0;\nend\n\n% find and load motion energy .mat file\nmepth = fullfile(datapth,'DataObjects',meta.anm);\ncontents = dir(mepth);\ncontents = contents(~[contents.isdir]);\n\nfns = {contents.name}';\n\nfn = patternMatchCellArray({contents.name}',{'motionEnergy',meta.date},'all');\n\nif numel(fn) == 1\n    fn = fn{1};\nelseif numel(fn) == 2\n    ix = ~contains(fn,'._'); % on windows, sometimes a file that starts with '._motionEnergy*' is found\n    fn = fn{ix};\nelse\n    disp('UNABLE TO LOCATE A MOTION ENERGY FILE IN: ')\n    disp(mepth)\n    disp('      Continuing without motion energy as a feature')\n    me.use = 0;\n    me.data = nan;\n    me.moveThresh = nan;\n    return\nend\n\ntemp = load(fullfile(mepth,fn));\nme = temp.me;\n\nif isstruct(me.data)\n    me.data = me.data.data;\nend\n\nif returnAfterLoad\n    return\nend\n\nvidshift = findVideoOffset(obj);\n% vidshift = 1;\n\n% -----------------------------------------------------------\n% trim trial length (me.data contains motion energy for each time point in\n% trial at 400 Hz). Want to align to params.alignEvent and want to put it\n% in same dt as neural data\n% -----------------------------------------------------------\ntaxis = obj.time + params.advance_movement;\nalignTimes = obj.bp.ev.(params.alignEvent);\nme.newdata = zeros(numel(obj.time),numel(me.data));\nfor trix = 1:numel(me.data)\n    try\n        me.newdata(:,trix) = interp1(obj.traj{1}(trix).frameTimes-vidshift-alignTimes(trix),me.data{trix},taxis);\n    catch % if frameTimes doesn't exist or is full of NaNs\n        frameTimes = (1:size(obj.traj{1}(trix).ts,1)) ./ 400;\n        me.newdata(:,trix) = interp1(frameTimes-0.5-alignTimes(trix),me.data{trix},taxis);\n    end\nend\n\n% replace me.data with me.newdata\nme.data = me.newdata;\nme = rmfield(me,'newdata');\n% fill nans with nearest value (there are some nans at the start of each trial)\nme.data = fillmissing(me.data,'nearest');\n\n% -------------------------------------------------------------------\n% -- assign move time points as logical array same size as me.data --\n% -------------------------------------------------------------------\nme.move = me.data > (me.moveThresh);\nend\n```\n\nLoads and processes motion energy from video:\n- Searches for `.mat` file matching animal name and session date\n- Interpolates from native 400 Hz video rate to neural data sampling rate (200 Hz)\n- Aligns to the trial alignment event using `findVideoOffset`\n- Creates logical `me.move` array where `me.data > me.moveThresh`\n- Fills NaNs at trial start with nearest value\n\n---\n\n## 9. VIDEO OFFSET CALCULATION - findVideoOffset.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/findVideoOffset.m`\n\n```matlab\n% find offset between neural data file start and video file start\nfunction vidshift = findVideoOffset(obj)\nbitStart = mode(obj.bp.ev.bitStart);\nvidFileOffset = mode(obj.sglx.bitcode.bitstart) / obj.sglx.fs;\nvidshift = vidFileOffset - bitStart; % (s)\nend\n```\n\nComputes video-to-neural alignment offset by:\n- Getting most common `bitStart` time from behavior events\n- Getting most common video bitcode start time, normalized by sampling frequency\n- Computing offset in seconds\n\n---\n\n## 10. KINEMATICS COMPUTATION - getKinematics.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/getKinematics.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction kin = getKinematics(obj,me,params)\n\n% Check if obj already has kin\nif isfield(obj,'kin')\n    if isfield(params,'reload_kin')\n        if params.reload_kin\n            % get kinematics\n        else\n            return % kin = obj.kin\n        end\n    end\n    return % kin = obj.kin\nelse\n    % get kinematics\nend\n\n% Kinematics\n% (xdisp,ydisp,xvel,yvel) for each dlc feature\n[kin.dat,kin.featLeg,kin.nans] = getKinematicsFromVideo(obj,params);\n\n% Tongue angle and length\nif ismember('tongue',params.traj_features{1})\n    [ang,len] = getLickAngleAndLength(kin.featLeg,kin.dat,kin.nans);\n    kin.featLeg{end+1} = 'tongue_angle';\n    kin.featLeg{end+1} = 'tongue_length';\n    \n    kin.dat(:,:,end+1) = ang;\n    kin.dat(:,:,end+1) = len;\nend\n\n% Motion energy\nkin.featLeg{end+1} = 'motion_energy';\nkin.dat(:,:,end+1) = me.data;\n\n% Standardize kinematics\nfor featix = 1:size(kin.dat,3)\n    temp = kin.dat(:,:,featix);\n    temp2 = temp(:);\n    kin.dat_std(:,:,featix) = (temp - mean(temp2,'omitnan')) ./ std(temp2,'omitnan'); \nend\n\n% DIMENSIONALITY REDUCTION\n% Many video features will be highly correlated, so we perform PCA/FA\n% to reduce dimensionality to a set of factors that best explain the movement\nkin.dat_reduced = reduceDimensionVideoFeatures(kin.dat,params.feat_varToExplain);\nend\n```\n\nComprehensive kinematics processing pipeline:\n1. Extracts position and velocity for each DLC feature from video\n2. Computes tongue angle and length from multi-point tracking\n3. Includes motion energy from video\n4. Standardizes all features to z-score\n5. Applies PCA to reduce dimensionality\n\nDefault video features from getDefaultParams.m:\n```matlab\nparams.traj_features = {{'tongue','left_tongue','right_tongue','jaw','trident','nose'},...\n                        {'top_tongue','topleft_tongue','bottom_tongue','bottomleft_tongue','jaw','top_paw','bottom_paw','top_nostril','bottom_nostril'}};\nparams.feat_varToExplain = 80; % PCA components should explain 80% variance\nparams.advance_movement = 0.0;  % time offset for movement relative to neural data (s)\n```\n\n---\n\n### 10a. getKinematicsFromVideo.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/getKinematicsFromVideo.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [kin,featLeg,nans] = getKinematicsFromVideo(obj,params)\n\n% Parameters\nNvarsPerFeat = 4; % (xdisp,ydisp,xvel,yvel)\nfeats = params.traj_features; % features to extract kinematics for\ntaxis = obj.time + params.advance_movement; % time axis to align to\nnTrials = obj.bp.Ntrials;\n\n% Initialize position and velocity arrays\nxpos = cell(2,1); % one entry for each view\nypos = cell(2,1);\nxvel = cell(2,1);\nyvel = cell(2,1);\n\nfor viewix = 1:numel(feats) % loop through cam0 and cam1\n    xpos{viewix} = nan(numel(obj.time),nTrials);\n    ypos{viewix} = nan(numel(obj.time),nTrials);\n    xvel{viewix} = nan(numel(obj.time),nTrials);\n    yvel{viewix} = nan(numel(obj.time),nTrials);\n    for featix = 1:numel(feats{viewix}) % loop through features for current cam\n        feat = feats{viewix}{featix};\n\n        [xpos{viewix}(:,:,featix), ypos{viewix}(:,:,featix)] = findPosition(taxis, obj, nTrials, viewix, feat, params.alignEvent);\n        [xvel{viewix}(:,:,featix), yvel{viewix}(:,:,featix)] = findVelocity(xpos{viewix}(:,:,featix), ypos{viewix}(:,:,featix), feat);\n    end\nend\n\n[xpos, ypos, nans] = setTongueBaselinePosition(xpos,ypos,feats,nTrials);\n\n% Compute displacement and velocity matrix\nfeatMat = cell(2,1);\nviewNum = cell(2,1);\nfor viewix = 1:numel(feats) % loop through cam0 and cam1\n    featMat{viewix} = nan(size(xpos{viewix}, 1), size(xpos{viewix}, 2), size(xpos{viewix}, 3).*NvarsPerFeat);\n    for featix = 1:numel(feats{viewix}) % loop through features for current cam\n        tempx = xpos{viewix}(:,:,featix);\n        tempy = ypos{viewix}(:,:,featix);\n\n        % Feature matrix organization: [xdisp, ydisp, xvel, yvel] for each feature\n        featMat{viewix}(:,:,(featix*NvarsPerFeat)-3) = tempx;\n        featMat{viewix}(:,:,featix*NvarsPerFeat-2) = tempy;\n        featMat{viewix}(:,:,featix*NvarsPerFeat-1) = xvel{viewix}(:, :, featix);\n        featMat{viewix}(:,:,featix*NvarsPerFeat) = yvel{viewix}(:, :, featix);\n        viewNum{viewix}(featix) = viewix;\n    end\nend\n\n% Concatenate features into one big matrix of size (time, features*4, trials)\nkin = cat(3, featMat{:});\nv = cat(2, viewNum{:});\n\nallfeats = cat(2, feats{:});\nfeatLeg = cell(size(kin, 3), 1);\nfor i = 1:numel(allfeats)\n    featLeg{i*NvarsPerFeat-3} = [allfeats{i} '_xdisp_view' num2str(v(i))];\n    featLeg{i*NvarsPerFeat-2} = [allfeats{i} '_ydisp_view' num2str(v(i))];\n    featLeg{i*NvarsPerFeat-1} = [allfeats{i} '_xvel_view' num2str(v(i))];\n    featLeg{i*NvarsPerFeat} = [allfeats{i} '_yvel_view' num2str(v(i))];\nend\n\nend\n```\n\nReturns `kin` with shape `(time x trials x features*4)` containing all displacement and velocity components.\n\n---\n\n### 10b. findVelocity.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/findVelocity.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [xvel, yvel] = findVelocity(xpos, ypos, feat)\n\nfor i = 1:size(xpos,2) % for each trial\n    tsinterp = [xpos(:,i) ypos(:,i)];\n    basederiv = median(diff(tsinterp),'omitnan');\n\n    % find the difference between the feat velocity and the baseline feature velocity\n    xvel(:,i) = gradient(tsinterp(:,1));\n    yvel(:,i) = gradient(tsinterp(:,2));\n    \n    % For non-tongue features, subtract baseline drift\n    if ~contains(feat,'tongue')\n        xvel(:,i) = xvel(:,i) - basederiv(1);\n        yvel(:,i) = yvel(:,i) - basederiv(1);\n    end\n\n    % Fill missing values for all features\n    if ~contains(feat,'tongue')\n        xvel(:,i) = fillmissing(xvel(:,i),'nearest');\n        yvel(:,i) = fillmissing(yvel(:,i),'nearest');\n    else\n        tempx = xvel(:,i);\n        tempx(isnan(tempx)) = 0; % set tongue velocity to 0 if not visible\n        xvel(:,i) = tempx;\n        tempy = yvel(:,i);\n        tempy(isnan(tempy)) = 0; % set tongue velocity to 0 if not visible\n        yvel(:,i) = tempy;\n    end\nend\nend\n```\n\nComputes velocity using discrete gradient. For non-tongue features, subtracts baseline drift.\n\n---\n\n### 10c. findPosition.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/findPosition.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [xpos, ypos] = findPosition(taxis, obj, nTrials, view, feat, alignEv)\n\ntraj = obj.traj{view};\nfeatix = findDLCFeatIndex(obj.traj,view,{feat});\n\nxpos = nan(numel(taxis),nTrials);\nypos = nan(numel(taxis),nTrials);\n\nvidshift = findVideoOffset(obj);\n\nfor i = 1:nTrials % for each trial\n    trix = i;\n    \n    % check if video data for trial is good, skip if not\n    if isfield(traj(trix),'NdroppedFrames')\n        if isnan(traj(trix).NdroppedFrames )\n            continue;\n        end\n    end\n    \n    % check if frameTimes exists, if not, create it\n    if ~isfield(traj(trix),'frameTimes')\n        traj(trix).frameTimes = (1:size(traj(trix).ts,1)) ./ 400;\n    end\n    \n    % get feat trajectories for valid trials\n    if ~isnan(traj(trix).frameTimes)\n        ts = traj(trix).ts(:,1:2,featix);\n        % if tongue, don't smooth\n        if ~contains(feat,'tongue')\n            ts = mySmooth(ts, 1, 'reflect');\n        end\n        % linear interpolation of frameTimes to taxis\n        tsinterp = interp1(traj(trix).frameTimes-vidshift-obj.bp.ev.(alignEv)(trix), ts, taxis);\n    end\n    \n    xpos(:,i) = tsinterp(:,1);\n    ypos(:,i) = tsinterp(:,2);\n    \n    % fill missing values for all features except tongue\n    if ~contains(feat,'tongue')\n        xpos(:,i) = fillmissing(xpos(:,i),'nearest');\n        ypos(:,i) = fillmissing(ypos(:,i),'nearest');\n    end\nend\nend\n```\n\nExtracts DLC positions for a given feature across trials:\n- Skips trials with dropped frames\n- Interpolates from 400 Hz video rate to neural time axis\n- Aligns to trial event using video offset\n- Applies light smoothing (window=1) to non-tongue features only\n\n---\n\n### 10d. getLickAngleAndLength.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/getLickAngleAndLength.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [ang,len] = getLickAngleAndLength(featLeg,kin,nans)\n\n% Extract tongue coordinates from view 2 (bottom camera)\n[~,ix1] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'top_tongue_xdisp_view2'},'all');\nt1x = kin(:, :, ix1);\nt1x = setNans(t1x,'top_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix2] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'topleft_tongue_xdisp_view2'},'all');\nt2x = kin(:, :, ix2);\nt2x = setNans(t2x,'topleft_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix1] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'top_tongue_ydisp_view2'},'all');\nt1y = kin(:, :, ix1);\nt1y = setNans(t1y,'top_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix2] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'topleft_tongue_ydisp_view2'},'all');\nt2y = kin(:, :, ix2);\nt2y = setNans(t2y,'topleft_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix1] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'bottom_tongue_xdisp_view2'},'all');\nt3x = kin(:, :, ix1);\nt3x = setNans(t3x,'bottom_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix2] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'bottomleft_tongue_xdisp_view2'},'all');\nt4x = kin(:, :, ix2);\nt4x = setNans(t4x,'bottomleft_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix1] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'bottom_tongue_ydisp_view2'},'all');\nt3y = kin(:, :, ix1);\nt3y = setNans(t3y,'bottom_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix2] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'bottomleft_tongue_ydisp_view2'},'all');\nt4y = kin(:, :, ix2);\nt4y = setNans(t4y,'bottomleft_tongue',nans);\n\n[~,ix1] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'jaw_xdisp_view2'},'all');\n[~,ix2] = patternMatchCellArray(featLeg,{'jaw_ydisp_view2'},'all');\njx = kin(:, :, ix1);\njy = kin(:, :, ix2);\n\n% Compute tongue tip and base positions as averages of tracked points\ntongue_tip_x = 0.5.*(t1x+t3x);   % average of top and bottom tongue points\ntongue_tip_y = 0.5.*(t1y+t3y);\n\ntongue_base_x = 0.5.*(t2x+t4x);  % average of topleft and bottomleft tongue points\ntongue_base_y = 0.5.*(t2y+t4y);\n\n% Compute angle and length relative to jaw\ndx = (tongue_tip_x-jx);\ndy = (tongue_tip_y-jy);\nang = atan(dy./dx);\nang(dx<0 & dy>0) = ang(dx<0 & dy>0) + pi;     % Quadrant correction\nang(dx<0 & dy<0) = ang(dx<0 & dy<0) - pi;\nlen = sqrt(dx.^2 + dy.^2);\nend\n```\n\nComputes tongue angle and length (extension) relative to jaw position.\n\n---\n\n### 10e. reduceDimensionVideoFeatures.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/funcs/kinematics/reduceDimensionVideoFeatures.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction feats_reduced = reduceDimensionVideoFeatures(feats,varToExplain)\n\nfeats_rsz = reshape(feats, size(feats, 1)*size(feats, 2), size(feats, 3));   % (time*trials x feats)\n\n% how many PCs needed to explain target variance\nfeats_rsz = fillmissing(feats_rsz,'nearest');\nfeats_rsz(isnan(feats_rsz)) = 0;\n[~, ~, ~, ~, explained] = pca(feats_rsz);\nnumFactors = numComponentsToExplainVariance(explained, varToExplain);\n\n[~,feats_reduced]  = pca(feats_rsz,'NumComponents',numFactors);\nfeats_reduced = reshape(feats_reduced,size(feats,1),size(feats,2),size(feats_reduced,2));\nend\n```\n\nReduces video features via PCA to explain 80% variance (default).\n\n---\n\n## 11. SMOOTHING - mySmooth.m\n\n**File:** `/app/code/utils/mySmooth.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [out,kernsd] = mySmooth(x, N, varargin)\n% Gaussian kernel smoothing operating on first dimension only\n% varargin options:\n%   'reflect' - reflect N elements from beginning of time series across 0 index\n%   'zeropad' - pad N zeros across 0 index\n%   'none'    - don't handle boundary conditions\n\n% returns:\n% - out: filtered data, same size as x\n% - kernsd: std dev of gaussian kernel\n\nif N == 0 || N == 1 % no smoothing\n    out = x;\n    kernsd = 0;\n    return\nend\n\nif isrow(x) % if 1-d time series, ensure column vector\n    x = x';\nend\n\nif nargin > 2\n    % handle a boundary condition\n    bctype = varargin{1};\n    if strcmpi(bctype,'reflect')\n        x_filt = cat(1,x(1:N,:),x);\n        trim = N + 1;\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'zeropad')\n        x_filt = cat(1,zeros(N,size(x,2)),x);\n        trim = N + 1;\n    elseif strcmpi(bctype,'none')\n        x_filt = x;\n        trim = 1;\n    else\n        warning('invalid boundary condition type - regular smoothing');\n        x_filt = x;\n        trim = 1;\n    end\nelse\n    % no bc handling\n    x_filt = x;\n    trim = 1;\nend\n\nNcol = size(x_filt, 2);\nNel = size(x_filt, 1);\n\nkern = gausswin(N);\nkernsd = std(1:N);\n\nkern(1:floor(numel(kern)/2)) = 0; % CAUSAL kernel (only past samples)\nkern = kern./sum(kern);\n\nout = zeros(Nel, Ncol);\nfor j = 1:Ncol\n    out(:, j) = conv(x_filt(:, j), kern, 'same');\nend\nout = out(trim:end,:);\nend\n```\n\n**Key feature: CAUSAL smoothing** - Only uses past samples (first half of kernel set to 0)\n\nDefault parameters from getDefaultParams.m:\n```matlab\nparams.smooth = 15;    % gaussian kernel window size\nparams.bctype = 'none'; % boundary condition type\n```\n\n---\n\n## 12. RECORDING & VIDEO CONFIGURATION\n\nExample session loading scripts in `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/Recording and video/`:\n\n### loadEKH1_ALMVideo.m\n```matlab\nmeta(end+1).anm = 'EKH1';\nmeta(end).datapth = datapth;\nmeta(end).date = '2021-08-07';\nmeta(end).probe = 2;\n```\nShows that EKH1 used **Probe 2** on 2021-08-07.\n\n### loadJEB4_ALMVideo.m\n```matlab\nmeta(end+1).anm = 'JEB4';\nmeta(end).date = '2021-03-14';\nmeta(end).probe = 1;\n\nmeta(end+1) = meta(end);\nmeta(end).date = '2021-03-15';\nmeta(end).probe = 2;\n\nmeta(end+1) = meta(end);\nmeta(end).date = '2021-03-16';\nmeta(end).probe = 1;\n```\nShows that JEB4 had:\n- 2021-03-14: **Probe 1**\n- 2021-03-15: **Probe 2**\n- 2021-03-16: **Probe 1**\n\n### loadJGR2_ALMVideo.m\n```matlab\nmeta(end+1).anm = 'JGR2';\nmeta(end).date = '2021-11-16';\nmeta(end).probe = 1;\n\nmeta(end+1) = meta(end);\nmeta(end).date = '2021-11-17';\nmeta(end).probe = 1;\n```\nShows that JGR2 used **Probe 1** on both days.\n\n---\n\n## 13. HELPER FUNCTIONS\n\n### getStructVarNames.m\n**File:** `/app/code/utils/getStructVarNames.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction varnames = getStructVarNames(obj)\n\nf = obj.bp;\nfnames = fieldnames(f);\nNtrials = f.Ntrials;\n\nvarnames = {};\n\nfor i = 1:numel(fnames)\n    if numel(f.(fnames{i}))==Ntrials && ~iscell(f.(fnames{i}))\n        varnames{end+1} = fnames{i};\n    else\n        thisvar = f.(fnames{i});\n        if isstruct(thisvar)\n            morefnames = fieldnames(thisvar);\n            for j = 1:numel(morefnames)\n                if numel(thisvar.(morefnames{j}))==Ntrials && ~iscell(thisvar.(morefnames{j}))\n                    varnames{end+1} = [fnames{i} '.' morefnames{j}];\n                end\n            end\n        end\n    end\nend\nend\n```\n\nExtracts all trial-by-trial variables from behavioral structure.\n\n### patternMatchCellArray.m\n**File:** `/app/code/utils/patternMatchCellArray.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [list,mask] = patternMatchCellArray(list,patterns,whichPatterns)\n\nif strcmpi(whichPatterns,'all')\n    fun = @(s)~cellfun('isempty',strfind(list,s));\n    out = cellfun(fun,patterns,'UniformOutput',false);\n    mask = all(horzcat(out{:}),2);\nelseif strcmpi(whichPatterns,'any')\n    mask = ismember(lower(list), lower(patterns));\nend\n\nlist = {list{mask}}';\nend\n```\n\nFinds cells in array matching patterns (case-insensitive).\n\n### findDataFn.m\n**File:** `/app/code/utils/findDataFn.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction fn = findDataFn(meta)\nobjpth = fullfile(meta.datapth,'DataObjects',meta.anm); \ncontents = dir(objpth);\ncontents = contents(~[contents.isdir]);\n\nfns = {contents.name}';\n\nfn = patternMatchCellArray({contents.name}',{'data_structure',meta.date},'all');\nif isempty(fn)\n    fn = patternMatchCellArray({contents.name}',{meta.datafn},'all');\nend\n\nif numel(fn) == 1\n    fn = fn{1};\nelseif numel(fn) == 2\n    ix = ~contains(fn,'._');\n    fn = fn{ix};\nelse\n    error(['Found multiple data_structures containing strings: ' meta.anm ' and/or ' meta.date ' in: ' objpth]);\nend\nend\n```\n\nLocates data file matching animal name and session date.\n\n### baselineFR.m\n**File:** `/app/code/DataLoadingScripts/baselineFR.m`\n\n```matlab\nfunction [presampleFR, presampleSigma] = baselineFR(obj,params,prbnum)\n\n% baselineFR - (nCells,nTrialTypes) mean FR for each cluster and trial type\n\nedges = -0.5:params.dt:mode(obj.bp.ev.sample);\ntime = edges + params.dt/2;\ntime = time(1:end-1);\n\n% get psths by condition\npsth = zeros(numel(time),numel(params.cluid{prbnum}),numel(params.condition));\nfor i = 1:numel(params.cluid{prbnum})\n    curClu = params.cluid{prbnum}(i);\n    for j = 1:numel(params.condition)\n        trix = params.trialid{j};\n        spkix = ismember(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trial, trix);\n\n        N = histc(obj.clu{prbnum}(curClu).trialtm(spkix), edges);\n        N = N(1:end-1);\n\n        psth(:,i,j) = mySmooth(N./numel(trix)./params.dt, params.smooth);\n    end\nend\n\npresampleFR = squeeze(median(psth,1));\npresampleSigma = squeeze(std(psth,[],1));\nend\n```\n\nComputes baseline firing rates during presample epoch (from stimulus start to go cue).\n\n---\n\n## 14. KEY PARAMETER SUMMARY\n\nFrom `getDefaultParams.m`:\n\n```matlab\n% Alignment and timing\nparams.alignEvent = 'goCue';           % alignment event for spike rasters\nparams.tmin = -2.5;                    % start time relative to align event (s)\nparams.tmax = 2.5;                     % end time relative to align event (s)\nparams.dt = 1/200;                     % bin size = 5 ms\nparams.lowFR = 0.5;                    % min FR threshold (spikes/s)\n\n% Smoothing\nparams.smooth = 15;                    % gaussian kernel window (15 bins = 75 ms)\nparams.bctype = 'none';                % boundary condition type\n\n% Trial conditions (logical expressions evaluated against obj.bp fields)\nparams.condition(1) = {'R&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'};\nparams.condition(2) = {'L&hit&~stim.enable&~autowater&~early'};\nparams.condition(3) = {'R&hit&~stim.enable&autowater&~early'};\nparams.condition(4) = {'L&hit&~stim.enable&autowater&~early'};\n\n% Cluster quality\nparams.quality = {'all'};               % clusters to include (excludes: garbage, noisy, real?)\n\n% Probes and areas\nparams.probe(1) = 1;\nparams.probeArea{1} = 'ALM';\n\n% Video features (for each camera view)\nparams.traj_features = {\n    {'tongue','left_tongue','right_tongue','jaw','trident','nose'},\n    {'top_tongue','topleft_tongue','bottom_tongue','bottomleft_tongue','jaw','top_paw','bottom_paw','top_nostril','bottom_nostril'}\n};\n\n% Dimensionality reduction\nparams.feat_varToExplain = 80;          % PCA components explain 80% variance\n\n% Movement/video time offsets\nparams.prep = [-2.5 -0.05];             % prep epoch (s)\nparams.move = [-2.5 1.5];               % move epoch (s)\nparams.advance_movement = 0.0;          % video lead time (s)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 15.
